But... that's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been told by a doctor that you have any brain or nerve problem?\n\nPatient: No... no, nobody ever said nothing about that. Telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: When this happens, do you hear an actual voice speaking when no one is there?\n\nPatient: Well... sometimes. Yeah, I suppose so. Some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet... I hear it. Like my old friend calling my name from the porch. Or... or humming a choir song. Sounds clear. Just like he's standing right there. But... that's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been told by a doctor that you have any brain or nerve problem?\n\nPatient: No... no, nobody ever said nothing about that. Just my regular checkups, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually high or full of energy for days at a time?\n\nPatient: Well... I suppose there are times like that. Weeks where I wake up just... feeling real bright. Important, maybe. I guess I laugh a bit louder, talk faster than usual. But... well, if folks try to slow me down, or ask too many questions... I can get a bit sharp with them. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages and voices happen only when you’re in those bright, high-energy periods?\n\nPatient: No... not just then. There was a spell... maybe a month or so, where things were quiet. I was sleeping fine, not doing much of anything special. But... I still heard him. And the radio... it still seemed like it was talking to me. Even when I wasn't feeling all... worked up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that someone was putting thoughts into your mind or taking thoughts out of it?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. My thoughts are my own. Just... regular thoughts. Nobody's messing with them.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been going on for more than a month?\n\nPatient: Well... yes. It's been a while now. I suppose... maybe nine months or so. On and off. They just... come and go. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Since these experiences began, have you had trouble keeping up with your usual work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Well... yes, I suppose. I haven't been going to choir practice much. And... the garden's gotten a bit away from me. I used to play dominoes with the fellas, but... it just got uncomfortable, so I don't go around for that anymore. Just... haven't been keeping up with things like I used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any seizures?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I haven't had anything like that. But... that's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been told by a doctor that you have any brain or nerve problem?\n\nPatient: No... no, nobody ever said nothing about that. Just my regular checkups, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually high or full of energy for days at a time?\n\nPatient: Well... I suppose there are times like that. Weeks where I wake up just... feeling real bright. Important, maybe. I guess I laugh a bit louder, talk faster than usual. But... well, if folks try to slow me down, or ask too many questions... I can get a bit sharp with them. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages and voices happen only when you’re in those bright, high-energy periods?\n\nPatient: No... not just then. There was a spell... maybe a month or so, where things were quiet. I was sleeping fine, not doing much of anything special. But... I still heard him. And the radio... it still seemed like it was talking to me. It's been a while now. I suppose... maybe nine months or so. On and off. They just... come and go. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Since these experiences began, have you had trouble keeping up with your usual work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Well... yes, I suppose. I haven't been going to choir practice much. And... the garden's gotten a bit away from me. I used to play dominoes with the fellas, but... it just got uncomfortable, so I don't go around for that anymore. Just... haven't been keeping up with things like I used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any seizures?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I haven't had anything like that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, have you found that your emotions feel flatter or less strong than they used to?\n\nPatient: Well... I don't rightly know about flatter. I still love my family, of course. Just... a lot of days, I just sit at the kitchen table. Don't say much of anything. I let the garden and the choir stuff just... go by. Just don't feel like moving much, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe someone is trying to harm you?\n\nPatient: Harm? ... I don't rightly know about harm. But... well, I get these little messages. From the fellas at the domino table. And sometimes the radio announcer, too. \n\nMy wife... she says it's just regular talk. But I know better. I know they're warning me. Telling me I need to be careful. That's... well, that's why I came in, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Before these messages and voices started, did you begin any new medicines or start using alcohol or drugs more than usual?\n\nPatient: No... no new medicines. And I don't drink. Never have been one for drugs or any of that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stroke?\n\nPatient: No... not that I know of. Never had a stroke.\n\nDoctor: Have you been seeing things that other people cannot see?\n\nPatient: See things? No... no, I don't see nothing like that. \n\nJust... well, hearing, I suppose. There was a spell... maybe a month or so, where things were quiet. I was sleeping fine, not doing much of anything special. But... I still heard him. And the radio... it still seemed like it was talking to me. Even when I wasn't feeling all... worked up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that someone was putting thoughts into your mind or taking thoughts out of it?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. My thoughts are my own. Just... regular thoughts. Nobody's messing with them.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been going on for more than a month?\n\nPatient: Well... yes. It's been a while now. I suppose... maybe nine months or so. On and off. They just... come and go. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Since these experiences began, have you had trouble keeping up with your usual work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Well... yes, I suppose. I haven't been going to choir practice much. And... the garden's gotten a bit away from me. I used to play dominoes with the fellas, but... it just got uncomfortable, so I don't go around for that anymore. Just... haven't been keeping up with things like I used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any seizures?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I haven't had anything like that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, have you found that your emotions feel flatter or less strong than they used to?\n\nPatient: Well... I don't rightly know about flatter. I still love my family, of course. Just... a lot of days, I just sit at the kitchen table. Don't say much of anything. I let the garden and the choir stuff just... go by. Just don't feel like moving much, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe someone is trying to harm you?\n\nPatient: Harm? ... I don't rightly know about harm. But... well, I get these little messages. From the fellas at the domino table. And sometimes the radio announcer, too. \n\nMy wife... she says it's just regular talk. But I know better. I know they're warning me. Telling me I need to be careful. That's... well, that's why I came in, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Before these messages and voices started, did you begin any new medicines or start using alcohol or drugs more than usual?\n\nPatient: No... no new medicines. And I don't drink. Never have been one for drugs or any of that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stroke?\n\nPatient: No... not that I know of. Never had a stroke.\n\nDoctor: Have you been seeing things that other people cannot see?\n\nPatient: See things? No... no, I don't see nothing like that. \n\nJust... well, hearing, I suppose. I don't much like talking on it, but... some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. Calling my name from out on the porch. Or humming a choir song. Sounds clear, like he's standing right there. But... I don't see him. Just hear him.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having trouble with your memory?\n\nPatient: My memory? No... not really. I remember things fine, I suppose. Mind still works alright. Just... no trouble with that.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

Telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: When this happens, do you hear an actual voice speaking when no one is there?\n\nPatient: Well... sometimes. Yeah, I suppose so. Some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet... I hear it. Like my old friend calling my name from the porch. Or... or humming a choir song. Sounds clear. Just like he's standing right there. But... that's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been told by a doctor that you have any brain or nerve problem?\n\nPatient: No... no, nobody ever said nothing about that. Just my regular checkups, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually high or full of energy for days at a time?\n\nPatient: Well... I suppose there are times like that. Weeks where I wake up just... feeling real bright. Important, maybe. I guess I laugh a bit louder, talk faster than usual. But... well, if folks try to slow me down, or ask too many questions... I can get a bit sharp with them. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages and voices happen only when you’re in those bright, high-energy periods?\n\nPatient: No... not just then. There was a spell... maybe a month or so, where things were quiet. I was sleeping fine, not doing much of anything special. But... I still heard him. And the radio... it still seemed like it was talking to me. Even when I wasn't feeling all... worked up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that someone was putting thoughts into your mind or taking thoughts out of it?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. My thoughts are my own. Just... regular thoughts. Nobody's messing with them.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been going on for more than a month?\n\nPatient: Well... yes. It's been a while now. I suppose... maybe nine months or so. On and off. They just... come and go. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Since these experiences began, have you had trouble keeping up with your usual work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Well... yes, I suppose. I haven't been going to choir practice much. And... the garden's gotten a bit away from me. I used to play dominoes with the fellas, but... it just got uncomfortable, so I don't go around for that anymore. Just... haven't been keeping up with things like I used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any seizures?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I haven't had anything like that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, have you found that your emotions feel flatter or less strong than they used to?\n\nPatient: Well... I don't rightly know about flatter. I still love my family, of course. Just... a lot of days, I just sit at the kitchen table. Don't say much of anything. I let the garden and the choir stuff just... go by. Just don't feel like moving much, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe someone is trying to harm you?\n\nPatient: Harm? ... I don't rightly know about harm. But... well, I get these little messages. From the fellas at the domino table. And sometimes the radio announcer, too. \n\nMy wife... she says it's just regular talk. But I know better. I know they're warning me. Telling me I need to be careful. That's... well, that's why I came in, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Before these messages and voices started, did you begin any new medicines or start using alcohol or drugs more than usual?\n\nPatient: No... no new medicines. And I don't drink. Never have been one for drugs or any of that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stroke?\n\nPatient: No... not that I know of. Never had a stroke.\n\nDoctor: Have you been seeing things that other people cannot see?\n\nPatient: See things? No... no, I don't see nothing like that. \n\nJust... well, hearing, I suppose. I don't much like talking on it, but... some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. Calling my name from out on the porch. Or humming a choir song. Sounds clear, like he's standing right there. But... I don't see him. Just hear him.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having trouble with your memory?\n\nPatient: My memory? No... not really. I remember things fine, I suppose. Mind still works alright. Just... no trouble with that.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you felt confused or not fully clear-headed?\n\nPatient: No... can't say I have. Head's clear enough, mostly. I know where I am and all that. So... no.\n\nDoctor: Have you had trouble with your hearing?\n\nPatient: My hearing? No... ears work alright, I reckon. Catch most of what folks say. So... no trouble there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had spells where you suddenly smell an odor that isn’t really there?\n\nPatient: Odors? No... can't say I have. Nose is just... normal, I guess. Ain't smelled nothing that wasn't there. So... no.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, have you found yourself talking much less than you used to?\n\nPatient: I suppose... maybe a bit. I don't say much these days. Mostly just... sit at the kitchen table. I still care for my family, but... just don't feel like talking. Or doing much of anything, really. Let the garden go... stopped going to choir practice. Just... sit there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
